<ruby><rb>地理书</rb><rt>DI-LI-SHÜ</rt></ruby>

——<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>万国</rb><rt>VÆN-KOH</rt><rb>古今</rb><rt>KWU-KYING</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>Z-TʽI</rt><rb>有点</rb><rt>YIU-TIN</rt><rb>讲进</rb><rt>KÔNG-TSING</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>[1]</sup> 

相土所宜 利有攸往

以古为鉴 动罔不臧

<ruby><rb>卷</rb><rt>Kyün</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>lôh-væn</rt></ruby> 1852 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby><sup>[2]</sup> 

<ruby><rb>宁波</rb><rt>NYINGPO</rt></ruby>

<sup>\[1\] 现译为<u>意大利</u></sup>

<sup>\[2\] 即公元 1852 年</sup>

## <ruby><rb>第一</rb><rt>Di-ih</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有名头</rb><rt>yiu-ming-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>。

<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>当初子</rb><rt>tông-tsʽu-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>欧罗巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><u><ruby><rb>亚细亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>国数</rb><rt>koh-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大英</rb><rt>Da-Ing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚拉伯</rb><rt>Üô-lah-pah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿非利加</rb><rt>Ah-fi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>进贡个</rb><rt>tsing-kong-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>欧罗巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>墨西哥</rb><rt>Moh-si-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>南</rb><rt>Nen</rt><rb>亚美利加</rb><rt>Üô-me-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt></ruby><sup>\[4\]</sup><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>今难间</rb><rt>kying-næn-kæn</rt></ruby><sup>\[5\]</sup><ruby><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>大里</rb><rt>da-li</rt><rb>明白个</rb><rt>ming-bah-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 和；跟。现代宁波话说「搭」</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 现译为<u>阿拉伯</u></sup>

<sup>\[3\] 地方</sup>

<sup>\[4\] 时候</sup>

<sup>\[5\] 现在</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>苗子</rb><rt>miao-ts</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>话的</rb><rt>wô-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>迁</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西基里</rb><rt>Si-kyi-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>庉间</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>乡方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相像个</rb><rt>siang-ziang-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 土著</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Sicily，现译为<u>西西里</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>即如</rb><rt>Tsih-jü</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>处处</rb><rt>cʽü-cʽü</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>字眼</rb><rt>z-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>样式个</rb><rt>yiang-shih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>罗马字</rb><rt>Lo-mô-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇号</rb><rt>keh-ʽao</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>出个</rb><rt>cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>借来个</rb><rt>tsia-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>样</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>式</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>有点</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>改过</rb><rt>kæ-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>一大半</rb><rt>ih-do-pun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>希腊字</rb><rt>Hyi-lah-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>样式个</rb><rt>yiang-shih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>据</rb><rt>Kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>字眼</rb><rt>z-ngæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加达摩斯</rb><rt>Kô-dah-mo-s</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>——<ruby><rb>带过来个</rb><rt>ta-ko-læ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Κάδμος，现译为<u>卡德摩斯</u>。<u>希腊</u>神话人物，传说是他将腓尼基字母传入<u>希腊</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>样个</rb><rt>yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>希腊字</rb><rt>Hyi-lah-z</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt><rb>希伯来字</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-z</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话起来</rb><rt>wô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>西半边</rb><rt>Si-pun-pin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇号</rb><rt>keh-ʽao</rt><rb>罗马字</rb><rt>Lo-mô-z</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>亏了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜个</rb><rt>pa-go</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜个</rb><rt>pa-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>各样</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教门</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拉</rb><rt>lah</rt><rb>借过来个</rb><rt>tsia-ko-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗必</rb><rt>feh-pih</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 神仙</sup>

<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>二三百</rb><rt>nyi-sæn-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>Ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>缺个</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>无从</rb><rt>vu-dzong</rt><rb>考究</rb><rt>kʽao-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有点</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>又虚</rb><rt>yi-hyü</rt><rb>又实个</rb><rt>yi-jih-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 752 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马鲁</rb><rt>Lo-mô-lu</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>：<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>低低</rb><rt>ti-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>格</rb><rt>kah</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>跳过去了</rb><rt>tʽiao-ko-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马鲁</rb><rt>Lo-mô-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生气</rb><rt>sang-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捉</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>打煞了</rb><rt>tang-sah-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>coh-kwun</rt><rb>动</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>干戈</rb><rt>ken-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Romulus，现译为<u>罗慕路斯</u>。传说中罗马城的创建者</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 常常</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>草寇</rb><rt>tsʽao-kʽeo</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>四凸乱圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>自<sup>\[2\]</sup>个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>许拨</rb><rt>he-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>忖出</rb><rt>tsʽeng-cʽih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>约定</rb><rt>iah-ding</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摆</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>擂台</rb><rt>le-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差去</rb><rt>tsʽa-kyʽi</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>四近</rb><rt>s-gying</rt><rb>四远</rb><rt>s-yün</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 到处</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 自己，现代宁波话说「自家」</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 计策</sup>

<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>男女</rb><rt>nen-nyü</rt><rb>老少</rb><rt>lao-siao</rt><rb>拢总走拢来</rb><rt>long-tsong-tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大班</rb><rt>ih-do-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>有余不剩</rb><rt>yiu-yü-peh-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>抢了</rb><rt>tsʽiang-leh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>抢去</rb><rt>Tsʽiang-kyʽi</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起了</rb><rt>kyʽi-leh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>两边</rb><rt>Liang-pin</rt><rb>阵势</rb><rt>dzing-shü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>摆开</rb><rt>pa-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>抢去间</rb><rt>tsʽiang-kyʽi-kæn</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>当中央</rb><rt>tông-cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>和止</rb><rt>ʽo-ts</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一边</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>打赢</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>伤心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若话</rb><rt>ziah-wô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>本地人</rb><rt>peng-di-nying</rt><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若话</rb><rt>ziah-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>打败</rb><rt>tang-ba</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>两边</rb><rt>Liang-pin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从此之后</rb><rt>dzong-tsʽ-ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>合带拢</rb><rt>ʽeh-tæn-long</rt><rb>做家生</rb><rt>tso-kô-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>自家</rb><rt>zi-kô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲了来</rb><rt>kông-deh-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 听见，现代宁波话说「听明」</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马鲁</rb><rt>Lo-mô-lu</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby> 37 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>罗马鲁</rb><rt>Lo-mô-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死过之</rb><rt>si-ko-ts</rt><rb>后</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>诸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄权</rb><rt>long-gyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>coh-kwun</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>诸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>治压</rb><rt>djü-ah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>公举</rb><rt>kong-kyü</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>才情</rb><rt>dzæ-dzing</rt><rb>好点</rb><rt>hao-tin</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>努马</rb><rt>Nu-mô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文错印为 Lo-mo-lu</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Numa Pompilius，罗马王政时期第二任国王</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>努马</rb><rt>Nu-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有点</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希腊</u>国</rb><rt>Hyi-lah-koh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>梭伦</rb><rt>So-leng</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>新作出</rb><rt>sing-tsôh-cʽih</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>仙人</rb><rt>sin-nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量仔</rb><rt>siang-liang-ts</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>倚重其</rb><rt>i-djong-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Σóλων。古希腊改革家、政治家</sup>

<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一边</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt><rb>着戤</rb><rt>dziah-gæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>有点</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>塑出</rb><rt>su-cʽih</rt><rb>菩萨个</rb><rt>bu-sah-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>隔壁</rb><rt>kah-pih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一国</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>两支</rb><rt>Liang-ts</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>会拢</rb><rt>we-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗舍得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>输赢</rb><rt>shü-ying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>原是</rb><rt>nyün-z</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>做主个</rb><rt>tso-cü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>能可</rb><rt>neng-kʽo</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>英雄</rb><rt>ing-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>对手</rb><rt>te-siu</rt><rb>𧼱</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>高低</rb><rt>kao-ti</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>赢了</rb><rt>ying-de</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>马上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>降书</rb><rt>ʽông-shü</rt><rb>投伏</rb><rt>deo-voh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 较高下</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 第二人称复数，现代宁波话说「倷」</sup>

<ruby><rb>于是乎</rb><rt>Ü-z-wu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>诃莱西</rb><rt>Ho-læ-si</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>半边</rb><rt>pun-pin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>三兄弟</rb><rt>sæn-hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古莱西</rb><rt>Kwu-læ-si</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Horatii</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Curiatii</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>对手</rb><rt>te-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>本事</rb><rt>peng-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>枪</rb><rt>tsʽiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>战</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>诃莱西</rb><rt>Ho-læ-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>登时里</rb><rt>teng-z-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>打煞了</rb><rt>tang-sah-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>三兄弟</rb><rt>sæn-hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>和三</rb><rt>ʽo-sæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>受伤了</rb><rt>ziu-sông-liao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>荡边</rb><rt>Dông-pin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>诃莱西</rb><rt>Ho-læ-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>一计</rb><rt>ih-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假戏头</rb><rt>kô-hyi-deo</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>败仗</rb><rt>ba-tsiang</rt><rb>走了</rb><rt>tseo-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>Keh-pin</rt><rb>三兄弟</rb><rt>sæn-hyüong-di</rt><rb>一看其</rb><rt>ih-kʽen-gyi</rt><rb>倒想</rb><rt>tao-siang</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>歇</rb><rt>hyih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>下三</rb><rt>ʽô-sæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt><rb>紧紧</rb><rt>kying-kying</rt><rb>追上</rb><rt>tse-zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>式势</rb><rt>sih-shü</rt><rb>弗像</rb><rt>feh-ziang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>看仔</rb><rt>kʽen-ts</rt><rb>讨是</rb><rt>tʽao-z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赢</rb><rt>ying</rt><rb>到手</rb><rt>tao-siu</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>诃莱西</rb><rt>Ho-læ-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>头扭转</rb><rt>deo-nyin-cün</rt><rb>一看，前头</rb><rt>ih-kʽen-zin-deo</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古莱西</rb><rt>Kwu-læ-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>有限</rb><rt>yiu-ʽæn</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>追着</rb><rt>tse-djôh</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>伤</rb><rt>sông</rt><rb>来勒</rb><rt>læ-leh</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>勒</rb><rt>leh</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>赶着</rb><rt>ken-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>诃莱西</rb><rt>Ho-læ-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>单等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>近拢来</rb><rt>gying-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从先</rb><rt>dzong-sin</rt><rb>倒奔转来</rb><rt>tao-peng-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出其不意</rb><rt>cʽih-gyi-peh-i</rt><rb>提起</rb><rt>di-kyʽi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>一刀</rb><rt>ih-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>结果</rb><rt>kyih-ko</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>Di-sæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>重伤了</rb><rt>djong-sông-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶着</rb><rt>ken-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>呵鼾</rb><rt>hô-hen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打其赢</rb><rt>tang-gyi-ying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>蛮省力个</rb><rt>m̆æn-sang-lih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>连即</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>Keh-pin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>投降</rb><rt>deo-ʽông</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做里通外国</rb><rt>tso-li-tʽong-nga-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>统性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>灭掉了</rb><rt>mih-diao-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### <ruby><rb>问个</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>要紧</rb><rt>iao-kying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>暴时</rb><rt>bao-z</rt><rb>庉间</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>字眼</rb><rt>z-ngæn</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凭据</rb><rt>bing-kyü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>罗马字</rb><rt>Lo-mô-z</rt><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>出个</rb><rt>cʽih-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教门</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt><rb>故事</rb><rt>kwu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>考实</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>筑个</rb><rt>côh-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>几时</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>筑个</rb><rt>côh-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马鲁</rb><rt>Lo-mô-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>死过之后</rb><rt>si-ko-ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>即位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>何如</rb><rt>ʽo-jü</rt><rb>呀</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？

<sup>\[1\] 现代宁波话说「啥人」</sup>

## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>二</rb><rt>Nyi</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>以前</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-yi-zin</rt></ruby> 409 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>诸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>总管</rb><rt>Tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅典</rb><rt>Üô-tin</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>一任</rb><rt>ih-zing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 国王<u>塔克文</u>被废，<u>罗马</u>王政时代结束，发生于公元前 509 年，此处原文年份有误</sup>

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>不鲁多</rb><rt>Peh-lu-to</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>偷伴</rb><rt>tʽeo-bun</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>挽回转</rb><rt>w̆æn-we-cün</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>废掉个</rb><rt>fi-diao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>不鲁多</rb><rt>Peh-lu-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>当面前</rb><rt>tông-min-zin</rt><rb>杀其掉</rb><rt>sah-gyi-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Lucius Junius Brutus，罗马共和国第一任执政官</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 偷偷地；暗地里</sup>

<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>诸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>充军</rb><rt>cʽong-kyüing</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>报仇</rb><rt>pao-dziu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>敌国</rb><rt>dih-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>挑唆</rb><rt>tʽiao-so</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>发兵</rb><rt>fah-ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>本事</rb><rt>peng-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>又是</rb><rt>yi-z</rt><rb>切齿切恨</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽ-tsʽih-ʽeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨气</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>下家</rb><rt>ʽô-kô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>让</rb><rt>nyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>把帅</rb><rt>pô-sæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>一大队</rb><rt>ih-do-de</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>到<u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>tao-Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>声势</rb><rt>sing-shü</rt><rb>厉害勒紧</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-leh-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>诸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大夫</rb><rt>da-fu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>执意</rb><rt>tsih-i</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>睬</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>一大班</rb><rt>ih-do-pæn</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>弗相</rb><rt>feh-siang</rt><rb>干</rb><rt>ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城外</rb><rt>dzing-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>破进</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>快了</rb><rt>kwʽa-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大</rb><rt>ih-do</rt><rb>班</rb><rt>pæn</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>素</rb><rt>su</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>领头</rb><rt>Ling-deo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>一大班</rb><rt>ih-do-pæn</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>跪带落</rb><rt>gyü-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>磕头</rb><rt>kʽeh-deo</rt><rb>礼拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>挡弗起</rb><rt>tông-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>Ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>亏了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>救转</rb><rt>kyiu-cün</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>难保</rb><rt>næn-pao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>退转去</rb><rt>tʽe-cün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>怨声</rb><rt>ün-sing</rt><rb>满道</rb><rt>mun-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>空手来</rb><rt>kʽong-siu-læ</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt><rb>半路上</rb><rt>pun-lu-zông</rt><rb>怏</rb><rt>ông</rt><rb>早早</rb><rt>tsao-tsao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Gnaeus Marcius Coriolanus，<u>罗马</u>将军</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 本段讲述<u>罗马</u>-<u>沃尔西</u>战争（公元前 491–488 年）期间的一个传说</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>本国</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也是</rb><rt>yia-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>孝顺</rb><rt>hyiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>阿娘个</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>着重</rb><rt>dziah-djong</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>外国王</rb><rt>nga-koh-wông</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>打进</rb><rt>tang-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>Shü-li</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西伏剌</rb><rt>Si-vo-la</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>营寨里</rb><rt>ying-dza-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>行刺</rb><rt>ʽang-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>下家</rb><rt>ʽô-kô</rt><rb>抲着</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>外国王</rb><rt>nga-koh-wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>招出</rb><rt>tsiao-cʽih</rt><rb>同谋</rb><rt>dong-meo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>半句</rb><rt>pun-kyü</rt><rb>虚话</rb><rt>hyü-wô</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周捉</rb><rt>tsiu-coh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西伏剌</rb><rt>Si-vo-la</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伸开</rb><rt>sing-kʽæ</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>顺只</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>火炉</rb><rt>ho-lu</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和只</rb><rt>ʽo-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>𤈦落了</rb><rt>me-lôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>变色</rb><rt>pin-seh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>外国</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放了</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>承尔情</rb><rt>Dzing-ng-dzing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>老实</rb><rt>lao-jih</rt><rb>话向尔道</rb><rt>wô-hyiang-ng-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇株介个</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三百</rb><rt>sæn-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>罚咒过了</rb><rt>vah-tsiu-ko-liao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>起血心</rb><rt>kyʽi-hyüih-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>谋杀</rb><rt>meo-sah</rt><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>防备</rb><rt>bông-be</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>𢞕</rb><rt>hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>寒了</rb><rt>ʽen-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连忙</rb><rt>lin-mông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>退</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 公元前 508 年，<u>罗马</u>与 Clusium 的战争</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Gaius Mucius Scaevola，<u>罗马</u>传说中的英雄</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 谎话。现代宁波话说「乱话」</sup>

### <ruby><rb>问个</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-ko</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>废掉了</rb><rt>fi-diao-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>Dza-go</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>不鲁多</rb><rt>Peh-lu-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>哥里倭兰</rb><rt>Ko-li-o-læn</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>西伏剌</rb><rt>Si-vo-la</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？

<sup>\[1\] 原文错印为 Ko-li-ô-læn.</sup>

---

## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>阿非利加</rb><rt>Ah-fi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>突尼斯</rb><rt>Deh-nyi-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当初子</rb><rt>tông-tsʽu-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>大码头</rb><rt>do-mô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>迁</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Carthage，现译为<u>迦太基</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓尼基</rb><rt>Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>担纲</rb><rt>tæn-kông</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>外乡</rb><rt>nga-hyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>很</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>闹热</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人势</rb><rt>nying-shü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>强勒</rb><rt>gyiang-leh</rt><rb>人家个</rb><rt>nying-kô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>年代</rb><rt>nyin-dæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西基里</rb><rt>Si-kyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西基里</rb><rt>Si-kyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伽多奇利</rb><rt>Üô-gô-to-gyi-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>出其不意</rb><rt>cʽih-gyi-peh-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>箇向</rb><rt>keh-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>上岸</rb><rt>zông-ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放火</rb><rt>fông-ho</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>进路</rb><rt>tsing-lu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>退路</rb><rt>tʽe-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>只会</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>声势</rb><rt>sing-shü</rt><rb>只会</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>勇</rb><rt>üong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>Tang</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>四年</rb><rt>s-nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>从先</rb><rt>dzong-sin</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西基里</rb><rt>Si-kyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>打进</rb><rt>tang-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西班牙</rb><rt>Si-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Ἀγαθοκλῆς，<u>叙拉古</u>国王，公元前 310 年登陆北非进攻<u>迦太基</u>本土</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>时介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>有限</rb><rt>yiu-ʽæn</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有点</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>惧惮</rb><rt>gyü-dæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>气其弗过</rb><rt>kyʽi-gyi-feh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>寻事吵闹</rb><rt>zing-z-tsʽao-nao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>闲早子</rb><rt>ʽæn-tsao-ts</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>帮衬</rb><rt>pông-tsʽeng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>敌国</rb><rt>dih-koh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>起了兵</rb><rt>kyʽi-leh-ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>征服个</rb><rt>tsing-voh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西基里</rb><rt>Si-kyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>陆路里</rb><rt>Loh-lu-li</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>惯</rb><rt>kwæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>水师里</rb><rt>se-s-li</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>忖出</rb><rt>tsʽeng-cʽih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只打只</rb><rt>tsah-tang-tsah</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>钩子</rb><rt>keo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>下家</rb><rt>ʽô-kô</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>扎牢仔</rb><rt>tsah-lao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>船个船</rb><rt>jün-ko-jün</rt><rb>过对过</rb><rt>ko-te-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>岸上</rb><rt>ngen-zông</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>亏勒</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二伐</rb><rt>di-nyi-vah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>打赢</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>过海</rb><rt>ko-hæ</rt><rb>打进</rb><rt>tang-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一阵</rb><rt>ih-dzing</rt><rb>赢</rb><rt>ying</rt><rb>一阵</rb><rt>ih-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>破进</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt><rb>城</u>里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刚刚</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>救兵</rb><rt>kyiu-ping</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉戈尼亚</rb><rt>Lah-ko-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>总兵</rb><rt>cong-ping</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>让</rb><rt>nyiang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希腊</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lah-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>把帅</rb><rt>pô-sæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打上</rb><rt>tang-zông</rt><rb>一阵</rb><rt>ih-dzing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赢</rb><rt>ying</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>公子</rb><rt>kong-ts</rt><rb>少爷</rb><rt>siao-yia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>水师里</rb><rt>se-s-li</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>败仗</rb><rt>ba-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>投降</rb><rt>deo-ʽông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>进贡</rb><rt>tsing-kong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 极大地</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 264 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby> 23 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直到</rb><rt>dzih-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 241 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>才始</rb><rt>dzæ-s</rt><rb>讲和</rb><rt>kông-ʽo</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>。

<ruby><rb>讲和</rb><rt>Kông-ʽo</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby> 22 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>从先</rb><rt>dzong-sin</rt><rb>失约</rb><rt>shih-iah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>起了</rb><rt>kyʽi-leh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西班牙</rb><rt>Si-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立约</rb><rt>lih-iah</rt><rb>过了个</rb><rt>ko-liao-go</rt></ruby>——<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介时候</rb><rt>Ka-z-ʽeo</rt><rb>元帅</rb><rt>nyün-sæ</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汉尼巴</rb><rt>Hen-nyi-pô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>本事</rb><rt>peng-z</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>九岁</rb><rt>kyiu-shü</rt><rb>箇年</rb><rt>keh-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>州</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忘记</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人个</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying-go</rt><rb>怨气</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>调动</rb><rt>diao-dong</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>Lu-zông</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>顶高</rb><rt>ting-kao</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt><rb>四季</rb><rt>s-kyi</rt><rb>有雪个</rb><rt>yiu-shih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连即</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>斫柴</rb><rt>côh-za</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗照咋</rb><rt>feh-tsiao-dza</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>粮草</rb><rt>liang-tsʽao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>依路</rb><rt>i-lu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>蛮人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>驮了</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>呆大大</rb><rt>ngæ-do-do</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夹头夹脑</rb><rt>kah-deo-kah-nao</rt><rb>揞落来</rb><rt>kʽang-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>巧<sup>\[2\]</sup>了</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>退</rb><rt>tʽe</rt></ruby>。

Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大将</rb><rt>da-tsiang</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇杨</rb><rt>Si-pi-yiang</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>箇向</rb><rt>keh-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>大臣</rb><rt>da-dzing</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>飞报</rb><rt>fi-pao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汉尼巴</rb><rt>Hen-nyi-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>本国</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt><rb>要紧</rb><rt>iao-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汉尼巴</rb><rt>Hen-nyi-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗舍得个</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>势道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗得</rb><rt>feh-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>转去</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇杨</rb><rt>Si-pi-yiang</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打了</rb><rt>tang-leh</rt><rb>败仗</rb><rt>ba-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从先</rb><rt>dzong-sin</rt><rb>投降</rb><rt>deo-ʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罚出</rb><rt>vah-cʽih</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt>< <ruby><rb>菩萨个</rb><rt>Bu-sah-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>菜花</rb><rt>tsʽæ-hwô</rt><rb>铜</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>铸出个</rb><rt>cü-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>空心</rb><rt>kʽong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>猛猛</rb><rt>mang-mang</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>丢勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一时</rb><rt>ih-z</rt><rb>三刻</rb><rt>sæn-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>擂鼓</rb><rt>le-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敲</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>铜锣</rb><rt>dong-lo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>欢天</rb><rt>hwun-tʽin</rt><rb>喜地</rb><rt>hyi-di</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>亚伽多奇利</rb><rt>Üô-gô-to-gyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>围住</rb><rt>we-djü</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>弗舍得</rb><rt>feh-sô-teh</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>亲儿</rb><rt>tsʽing-ng</rt><rb>亲囡</rb><rt>tsʽing-nô</rt><rb>献拨</rb><rt>hyin-peh</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只会</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>买来</rb><rt>ma-læ</rt><rb>穷苦</rb><rt>gyüong-kwʽu</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>福礼</rb><rt>foh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就将</rb><rt>ziu-tsiang</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>多星</rb><rt>to-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>王孙</rb><rt>wông-seng</rt><rb>公子</rb><rt>kong-ts</rt><rb>少爷</rb><rt>siao-yia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>焚自</rb><rt>veng-zi</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩陆</u>菩萨</rb><rt>Mo-loh-bu-sah</rt><rb>𤈦煞了</rb><rt>me-sah-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>三百</rb><rt>sæn-pah</rt><rb>多星</rb><rt>to-sing</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>舍出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>焚自</rb><rt>veng-zi</rt><rb>𤈦煞了</rb><rt>me-sah-de</rt></ruby>。

### <ruby><rb>问个</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一边</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>起头个</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>生意</rb><rt>Sang-i</rt><rb>何如呀</rb><rt>ʽo-jü-yia</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>兵马</rb><rt>Ping-mô</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>属国</rb><rt>joh-koh</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>亚伽多奇利</rb><rt>Üô-gô-to-gyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打进</rb><rt>tang-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿非利加</rb><rt>Ah-fi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>仗</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>起因个</rb><rt>kyʽi-ing-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>计较</rb><rt>kyi-kao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>过海</rb><rt>Ko-hæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿非利加</rb><rt>Ah-fi-li-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>运气</rb><rt>yüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>几时</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>起头个</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>Tang</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>年代</rb><rt>nyin-dæ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>乱进来</rb><rt>lön-tsing-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>把帅</rb><rt>pô-sæ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>起头个</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>武艺子</rb><rt>vu-nyih-ts</rt><rb>何如</rb><rt>ʽo-jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>亏了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汉尼巴</rb><rt>Hen-nyi-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇个人</rb><rt>keh-go-nying</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>结局</rb><rt>kyih-gyüoh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>遭</rb><rt>tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>闹事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抄灭个</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咋管大</rb><rt>dza-kun-do</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>着</rb><rt>Dziah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>乡方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>Pa</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>西基里</rb><rt>Si-kyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>围住</rb><rt>we-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>求法</rb><rt>gyiu-fah</rt></ruby>？
