## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 196 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安底阿戈</rb><rt>En-ti-ah-ko</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打发</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>钦差</rb><rt>kyʽing-tsʽa</rt><rb>过来</rb><rt>ko-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>让出</rb><rt>nyiang-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>安底阿戈</rb><rt>En-ti-ah-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>让</rb><rt>nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汉尼巴</rb><rt>Hen-nyi-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>趁此</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽ</rt><rb>机会</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>挑唆</rb><rt>tʽiao-so</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>打败仔</rb><rt>Tang-ba-ts</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>夺了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该管<sup>\[2\]</sup>了</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Antiochus，前一章译作<u>安德约戈</u>，<u>塞琉古</u>帝国第六位国王<u>安条克</u>三世</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 统治；管辖</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>马基顿</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>第五</rb><rt>di-ng</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>帮衬</rb><rt>pông-tsʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>败仔</rb><rt>ba-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲和</rb><rt>kông-ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只得</rb><rt>tsih-teh</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>当头</rb><rt>tông-deo</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抲去</rb><rt>kʽô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>个</rb><rt>Lo-mô-go</rt><rb>属国了</rb><rt>joh-koh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 168 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Μακεδονία，现译为<u>马其顿</u></sup>

<sup>\[2\] Φίλιππος Ε΄，此处为<u>马其顿</u>国王<u>腓力</u>五世</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 人质</sup>

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>南边</rb><rt>Nen-pin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>特买<sup>\[1\]</sup>个</rb><rt>deh-ma-go</rt><rb>小国</rb><rt>siao-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总名</rb><rt>cong-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚该亚</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>趁此</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽ</rt><rb>机会</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥林多</rb><rt>Ko-ling-to</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt><rb>掳掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放火</rb><rt>fông-ho</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦大其</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-gyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>同年个</rb><rt>dong-nyin-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>Tsʽô-feh-to</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西班牙</rb><rt>Si-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该管了</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby> 45 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿及尔</rb><rt>Ah-gyih-r</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[4\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>求搿泰</rb><rt>Gyiu-geh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[5\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>谋杀了</rb><rt>meo-sah-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>救兵</rb><rt>kyiu-ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>杀掉了</rb><rt>sah-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>求搿泰</rb><rt>Gyiu-geh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>饿</rb><rt>ngo</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该管个</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-go</rt><rb>属国了</rb><rt>joh-koh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 好些个；好几个。现代宁波话说「特猛」</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Ἀχαΐα</sup>

<sup>\[3\] Κόρινθος，又译为<u>科林斯</u></sup>

<sup>\[4\] Algiers，现译为<u>阿尔及尔</u>，此处指北非古国<u>努米底亚</u>，在今<u>阿尔及利亚</u>北部</sup>

<sup>\[5\] Jugurtha，现译为<u>朱古达</u>。<u>努米底亚</u>国王</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>扬开</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三𡍲</rb><rt>sæn-tʽah</rt><rb>大洲</rb><rt>da-tsiu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>该管个</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄到</rb><rt>long-tao</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄到</rb><rt>long-tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>头顶</rb><rt>deo-teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>别国个</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>流出来</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>浇浇</rb><rt>kyiao-kyiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>田畈</rb><rt>din-pæn</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>外国</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>征服了</rb><rt>tsing-voh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>势道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>列王</rb><rt>lih-wông</rt><rb>废王</rb><rt>fi-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>本国</rb><rt>peng-koh</rt><rb>掌权</rb><rt>công-gyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>外国</rb><rt>nga-koh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>杀了</rb><rt>sah-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>呒数目账个</rb><rt>m-su-moh-tsiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒要紧</rb><rt>m-iao-kying</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>阿及尔</rb><rt>Ah-gyih-r</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>打个</rb><rt>tang-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>元帅</rb><rt>nyün-sæ</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>出身</rb><rt>cʽih-sing</rt><rb>来勒</rb><rt>læ-leh</rt><rb>低微</rb><rt>ti-vi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>性格</rb><rt>sing-keh</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>公子</rb><rt>kong-ts</rt><rb>出身</rb><rt>cʽih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>资质</rb><rt>ts-tsih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>功劳</rb><rt>kong-lao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>切齿</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽ</rt><rb>切恨</rb><rt>tsʽih-ʽeng</rt><rb>气其弗过</rb><rt>kyʽi-gyi-feh-ko</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Gaius Marius，<u>罗马</u>改革家</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Lucius Cornelius Sulla，现译为<u>苏拉</u>。<u>罗马</u>将军、政治家、军事家</sup>

<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依了</rb><rt>i-deh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>诸侯</rb><rt>cü-ʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>归勒</rb><rt>kwe-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄出</rb><rt>long-cʽih</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打赢仔</rb><rt>tang-ying-ts</rt><rb>仗</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>马略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>充军</rb><rt>cʽong-kyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>本都</rb><rt>Peng-tu</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>米替大底</rb><rt>Mi-tʽi-da-ti</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>马略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt><rb>起了</rb><rt>kyʽi-leh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>破进</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一连</rb><rt>ih-lin</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>夜</rb><rt>yia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>公侯</rb><rt>kong-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>呒数目账</rb><rt>m-su-moh-tsiang</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>弄权</rb><rt>long-gyün</rt><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>公举</rb><rt>kong-kyü</rt><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇年</rb><rt>keh-nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>吃醉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>潮热</rb><rt>dziao-nyih</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Πόντος</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Μιθριδάτης，现译为<u>米特里达梯</u>，此处为<u>本都</u>王国国王<u>米特里达梯</u>六世</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>马略</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-liah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赢仔</rb><rt>ying-ts</rt><rb>破进</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>城里向</rb><rt>dzing-li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨气</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>报仇</rb><rt>pao-dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杀掉个</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>数都</rb><rt>su-tu</rt><rb>数弗遍</rb><rt>su-feh-pin</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弄权</rb><rt>long-gyün</rt><rb>弄到</rb><rt>long-tao</rt><rb>三年</rb><rt>sæn-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>让位</rb><rt>nyiang-we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>白身</rb><rt>bah-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-Su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt></ruby> 80 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两句</rb><rt>liang-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>写勒</rb><rt>sia-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>报恩</rb><rt>pao-eng</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>厚</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>庉晌</rb><rt>Deng-zông</rt><rb>几年</rb><rt>kyi-nyin</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>大将</rb><rt>da-tsiang</rt><rb>掌权</rb><rt>công-gyün</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-dzông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>亚细亚</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>征服个</rb><rt>tsing-voh-go</rt><rb>国数</rb><rt>koh-su</rt><rb>统总</rb><rt>tʽong-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>破进</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>掳掠个</rb><rt>lo-liah-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>八百</rb><rt>pah-pah</rt><rb>多星</rb><rt>to-sing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在其内</rb><rt>dzæ-gyi-nen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 63 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Gnaeus Pompeius，现译为<u>庞培</u>。<u>罗马</u>军事家、政治家</sup>

<ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>二个</rb><rt>nyi-go</rt><rb>大将</rb><rt>da-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>年代</rb><rt>nyin-dæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>西半边</rb><rt>Si-pun-pin</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>打仗个</rb><rt>tang-tsiang-ko</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>西班牙</rb><rt>Si-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>佛兰西</rb><rt>veh-læn-si</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>日耳曼</rb><rt>Jih-r-mæn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>荷兰</rb><rt>ʽO-læn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>粗蛮</rb><rt>tsʽu-mæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有勇</rb><rt>yiu-yüong</rt><rb>无谋</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>兵法</rb><rt>ping-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百多</rb><rt>ih-pah-to</rt><rb>万</rb><rt>væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>掳掠来个</rb><rt>lo-liah-læ-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Gaius Julius Caesar，现译为<u>凯撒</u>。<u>罗马</u>军事家、政治家</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 现译为<u>法兰西</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>葛莱苏</rb><rt>Keh-læ-su</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>叙事</rb><rt>jü-z</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Marcus Licinius Crassus，现译为<u>克拉苏</u>。<u>罗马</u>军事家、政治家</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 富有</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>葛莱苏</rb><rt>Keh-læ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>败仔</rb><rt>ba-ts</rt><rb>逃</rb><rt>dao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>谋杀了</rb><rt>meo-sah-de</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>皇帝个</rb><rt>wông-ti-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>弗上</rb><rt>feh-zông</rt><rb>四年</rb><rt>s-nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>公侯</rb><rt>kong-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>凶手个</rb><rt>hyüong-siu-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>结了党</rb><rt>kyih-deh-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby> 44 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>公侯</rb><rt>Kong-ʽeo</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>戏文</rb><rt>hyi-veng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>掳掠来个</rb><rt>lo-liah-læ-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>情愿</rb><rt>dzing-nyün</rt><rb>帮衬</rb><rt>pông-tsʽeng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>外甥</rb><rt>nga-sang</rt><rb>报其个</rb><rt>pao-gyi-go</rt><rb>仇</rb><rt>dziu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 厌恶</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 给予</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>外甥</rb><rt>nga-sang</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>屋大维</rb><rt>Oh-da-vi</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大将</rb><rt>da-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安敦</rb><rt>En-teng</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>结了党</rb><rt>kyih-deh-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>阿妹</rb><rt>ah-me</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>公侯</rb><rt>kong-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安敦</rb><rt>En-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女王</rb><rt>nyü-wông</rt><rb>同室</rb><rt>dong-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>屋大维</rb><rt>Oh-da-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>安敦</rb><rt>En-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>败仔</rb><rt>ba-ts</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女王</rb><rt>nyü-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Gaius Octavius，<u>罗马</u>帝国第一任元首</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Marcus Antonius，现译为<u>安东尼</u>，<u>罗马</u>政治家、军事家</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>屋大维</rb><rt>Oh-da-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爵位</rb><rt>tsiah-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>号</rb><rt>ʽao</rt></ruby>：<u><ruby><rb>奥古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>尊重个</rb><rt>tseng-djong-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寄拜</rb><rt>kyi-pa</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爵位</rb><rt>tsiah-we</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>世传个</rb><rt>shü-djün-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>屋大</u><sup>\[2\]</sup>就</rb><rt>Oh-da-ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>第一个</rb><rt>di-ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Augustus，现译为<u>奥古斯都</u></sup>

<sup>\[2\] 原文漏了一个「维」</sup>

### <ruby><rb>问个</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>夺了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>马基顿</rb><rt>Mô-kyi-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>亚该亚</rb><rt>Üô-kæ-üô</rt></ruby></u>？<u><ruby><rb>阿及尔</rb><rt>Ah-gyih-r</rt></ruby></u>？<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>马略</rb><rt>Mô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西拉</rb><rt>Si-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>权</rb><rt>gyün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？

## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>庙</rb><rt>miao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>寂纳<sup>\[1\]</sup>庙</rb><rt>Dzih-neh-miao</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庙门</rb><rt>miao-meng</rt><rb>关了</rb><rt>kwæn-liao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庙门</rb><rt>miao-meng</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>开个</rb><rt>kʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Janus，现译为<u>雅努斯</u>。<u>罗马</u>宗教中的门神</sup>

<ruby><rb>自从</rb><rt>Z-dzong</rt><rb>建</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算来</rb><rt>sön-læ</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 752 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>庙门</rb><rt>miao-meng</rt><rb>笃笃</rb><rt>toh-toh</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>关了</rb><rt>kwæn-leh</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>暂时</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>关一关</rb><rt>kwæn-ih-kwæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连忙</rb><rt>lin-mông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇勒</rb><rt>Keh-leh</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>逐节</rb><rt>djoh-tsih</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>笃发之头</rb><rt>tôh-fah-ts-deo</rt><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>鄞</u>县</rb><rt>Nying-yün</rt><rb>介管</rb><rt>ka-kun</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>直等</rb><rt>Dzih-teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奥古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>冷冷</rb><rt>lang-lang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>火热个</rb><rt>ho-nyih-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>英吉利</rb><rt>Ing-kyih-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>四近四远</rb><rt>s-gying-s-yün</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>国打国</rb><rt>koh-tang-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>使唤</rb><rt>s-hwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby> 600 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>但也理</rb><rt>Dæn-Yiæ-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>四只</rb><rt>s-tsah</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比伦</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>波斯</rb><rt>Po-s</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四个</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>连牵</rb><rt>lin-kyʽin</rt><rb>兴起来</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凶色色</rb><rt>hyüong-seh-seh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劲道</rb><rt>gying-dao</rt><rb>比众不同</rb><rt>pi-cong-peh-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴里</rb><rt>cü-pô-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>呆</rb><rt>ngæ</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>铁个</rb><rt>tʽih-go</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样色</rb><rt>yiang-seh</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咬碎</rb><rt>ngao-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃剩个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踏糊</rb><rt>dah-wu</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt><rb>章里</rb><rt>tsông-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] 邻近</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Daniel，现译作<u>但以理</u>、<u>达尼尔</u>。《旧约圣经·但以理书》的作者</sup>

<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>第四</rb><rt>di-s</rt><rb>章里</rb><rt>tsông-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>呆大大</rb><rt>ngæ-do-do</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>手梗</rb><rt>siu-kwang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大腿</rb><rt>do-tʽe</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>菜花铜</rb><rt>tsʽæ-hwô-dong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>脚梗</rb><rt>kyiah-kwang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>铁</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>铁</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四个</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>连牵</rb><rt>lin-kyʽin</rt><rb>兴起来</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>）。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>四个</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>铁</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>强</rb><rt>gyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>铁</rb><rt>Tʽih</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>敲碎</rb><rt>kʽao-se</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抄灭</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>铁</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>和</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>强横</rb><rt>gyiang-wang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>并拢</rb><rt>ping-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>两下</rb><rt>liang-ʽô</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>胶粘</rb><rt>kao-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>铁</rb><rt>tʽih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>所立失道</rb><rt>sô-lih-shih-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>和弗拢个</rb><rt>ʽo-feh-long-go</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] 指《但以理书》第二章「巨人像的异梦」</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>句</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>讲完</rb><rt>kông-wun</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>属国</rb><rt>joh-koh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>猛洋洋</rb><rt>mang-yiang-yiang</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>杂七不剌</rb><rt>zeh-tsʽih-peh-la</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔候我弗齐</rb><rt>ng-ʽeo-ngô-feh-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>虽</rb><rt>se</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 浩瀚；广大</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>耶</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt><rb>稣</rb><rt>su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt></ruby> 68 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿多</rb><rt>Ah-to</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>杀杀掉</rb><rt>sah-sah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>日耳曼</rb><rt>Jih-r-mæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>阿多</rb><rt>Ah-to</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>阿多</rb><rt>Ah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>抵挡</rb><rt>ti-tông</rt><rb>弗住</rb><rt>feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>寻死</rb><rt>zing-si</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>风声</rb><rt>fong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看样</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>菲士巴三</rb><rt>Fi-z-pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>归根</rb><rt>Kwe-keng</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>菲士巴三</rb><rt>Fi-z-pô-sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打赢</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt></ruby> 193 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>羽林兵</rb><rt>yü-ling-ping</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>不蒂捺士</rb><rt>Peh-ti-nah-z</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做事体</rb><rt>tso-z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏糟糟</rb><rt>ông-tsao-tsao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>皇帝个</rb><rt>wông-ti-go</rt><rb>爵位</rb><rt>tsiah-we</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>多个</rb><rt>to-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt></ruby> 900,0000 <ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>登位</rb><rt>teng-we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>做了</rb><rt>Tso-leh</rt><rb>五个月</rb><rt>ng-ko-yüih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>谋皇</rb><rt>meo-wông</rt><rb>篡位</rb><rt>tsʽön-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>太平个</rb><rt>tʽa-bing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>缺个</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>三百</rb><rt>Sæn-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>统总</rb><rt>tʽong-tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒有</rb><rt>tao-yiu</rt><rb>廿八</rb><rt>nyiæn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>杀掉个</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>凶恶个</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>Yü-to</rt><rb>十五</rb><rt>jih-ng</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>伤了</rb><rt>sông-leh</rt><rb>阵里个</rb><rt>dzing-li-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>算来</rb><rt>sön-læ</rt><rb>笃笃</rb><rt>toh-toh</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>六七个</rb><rt>loh-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>好点</rb><rt>hao-tin</rt><rb>主顾是</rb><rt>cü-kwu-z</rt><rb>善终个</rb><rt>jün-cong-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>loh-væn</rt></ruby> 287 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提贺葛里相</rb><rt>Di-ʽo-keh-li-siang</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>拔上</rb><rt>bah-zông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>do-kô</rt><rb>坐位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>皇帝了</rb><rt>wông-ti-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奥古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>挑选</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爵位</rb><rt>tsiah-we</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>一等</rb><rt>ih-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>治</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一国</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奥古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。

<ruby><rb>庉晌</rb><rt>Deng-zông</rt><rb>几年</rb><rt>kyi-nyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奥古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>退位</rb><rt>tʽe-we</rt><rb>让</rb><rt>nyiang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>平头</rb><rt>bing-deo</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>Tong-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>贡旦定</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>即位</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>合拢</rb><rt>ʽah-long</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>迁都</rb><rt>tsʽin-tu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>他大尼里</rb><rt>Tʽa-da-nyi-li</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>筑</rb><rt>côh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>贡旦定城</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding-dzing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>均士淡丁</rb><rt>kyüing-z-dæn-ting</rt></ruby></u>。

### <ruby><rb>问个</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>寂纳</rb><rt>Dzih-neh</rt><rb>庙</rb><rt>miao</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>关个</rb><rt>kwæn-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>开个</rb><rt>kʽæ-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>Koh-veng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>先知</rb><rt>Sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>但也理</rb><rt>Dæn-yiæ-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>懦弱</rb><rt>no-ziah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 68 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？196 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？300 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>谋杀</rb><rt>meo-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>善终</rb><rt>jün-cong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>提贺葛里相</rb><rt>Di-ʽo-keh-li-siang</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>贡旦定</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>贡旦定城</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding-dzing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>Tong-pin</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>谁拉</rb><rt>jü-lah</rt><rb>夺去</rb><rt>deh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>西边</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>分了</rb><rt>feng-leh</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>？

## <ruby><rb>第六</rb><rt>Di-Loh</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>头早仔</rb><rt>deo-tsao-ts</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>厚</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>德气</rb><rt>teh-kyʽi</rt><rb>爽性</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 美德</sup>

<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>打勒</rb><rt>tang-leh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>越弄</rb><rt>yüih-long</rt><rb>越凶</rb><rt>yüih-hyüong</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>太平个</rb><rt>tʽa-bing-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>真刀</rb><rt>tsing-tao</rt><rb>真枪</rb><rt>tsing-tsʽiang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>人阵</rb><rt>nying-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>认认</rb><rt>nying-nying</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>养间</rb><rt>iang-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>斗</rb><rt>teo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>男男</rb><rt>nen-nen</rt><rb>女女</rb><rt>nyü-nyü</rt><rb>看个</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>拖吃</rb><rt>tʽa-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>对手</rb><rt>te-siu</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>看东</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>只会</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>掴脚</rb><rt>kwah-kyiah</rt><rb>掴手</rb><rt>kwah-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呵啰大笑</rb><rt>ho-lo-da-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>高兴相</rb><rt>kao-hying-siang</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>da</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>潘沛</rb><rt>Pʽun-pʽe</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 500 <ruby><rb>多星</rb><rt>to-sing</rt><rb>狮子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大拉象</rb><rt>Da-lah-ziang</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby> 1,0000 <ruby><rb>野兽</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby> 1,0000 <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>提了</rb><rt>di-leh</rt><rb>刀枪</rb><rt>tao-tsʽiang</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>革老丢</rb><rt>Keh-lao-tiu</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 1,9000 <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>认真话</rb><rt>nying-tsing-wô</rt><rb>像两国</rb><rt>ziang-liang-koh</rt><rb>交战</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>嬉头</rb><rt>hyi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>心焦煞了</rb><rt>sing-tsiao-sah-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>tsih-kwun</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Trajan，现译为<u>图拉真</u></sup>

<sup>\[2\] Claudius，现译为<u>克劳狄</u>。此处为<u>克劳狄</u>一世</sup>

<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还要</rb><rt>wa-iao</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>借过来</rb><rt>tsia-ko-læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>将军</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚基薄</rb><rt>Üô-kyi-bôh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>呆大大</rb><rt>ngæ-do-do</rt><rb>庙宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>都神殿</rb><rt>Tu-jing-din</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Agrippa，现译为<u>阿格里帕</u>，罗马政治家、军人</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 现译为<u>万神殿</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>Bu-sah</rt><rb>越</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真心</rb><rt>tsing-sing</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>越发</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>缺了</rb><rt>kyʽüih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>人只会</rb><rt>nying-tsih-we</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>顶坏</rb><rt>ting-w̆a</rt><rb>坏坯子</rb><rt>w̆a-pʽe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>顶坏</rb><rt>ting-w̆a</rt><rb>坏乡方</rb><rt>w̆a-hyiang-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>流落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>窞里</rb><rt>den-li</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>坏是</rb><rt>w̆a-z</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>到极头</rb><rt>tao-gyih-deo</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>罗马书</rb><rt>Lo-mô-shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>第一章</rb><rt>di-ih-tsông</rt></ruby>）。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>并弗是</rb><rt>bing-feh-z</rt><rb>弗晓得</rb><rt>feh-hyiao-teh</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就话</rb><rt>ziu-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>圣贤</rb><rt>sing-yin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>做人个</rb><rt>tso-nying-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>圣贤</rb><rt>sing-yin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>M-kao</rt><rb>惧惮</rb><rt>gyü-dæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>呒法</rb><rt>m-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>巧勒</rb><rt>kyʽiao-leh</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>教人</rb><rt>kao-nying</rt><rb>德行</rb><rt>teh-ʽang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奥古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>第</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby> 17 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>成为</rb><rt>dzing-we</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>」，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>以马努力</rb><rt>Yi-mô-nu-lih</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>」。<ruby><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神</rb><rt>Jing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>直</rb><rt>Dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>四五十</rb><rt>s-ng-jih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>行教</rb><rt>ʽang-kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>个</rb><rt>Lo-mô-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>公会</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>已经兴旺</rb><rt>yi-kying-hying-wông</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Emmanuel，又译为以马内利。希伯来语，意为「上帝与我们同在」</sup>

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼罗</rb><rt>Nyi-lo</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>了弗得</rb><rt>liao-feh-teh</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>了弗得</rb><rt>liao-feh-teh</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>据</rb><rt>Kyü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>放了</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt><rb>京城</rb><rt>kying-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>推拨</rb><rt>tʽe-peh</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>冤枉</rb><rt>ün-wông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>抲来</rb><rt>kʽô-læ</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>信差</rb><rt>sing-tsʽa</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><u><ruby><rb>保罗</rb><rt>Pao-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在其内</rb><rt>dzæ-gyi-nen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>花园</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>蜡烛</rb><rt>lah-coh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Nero，现译为<u>尼禄</u>，<u>罗马</u>皇帝之一</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Apostle，指基督教中为了某个特殊使命奉召被差派出去的门徒，后译为使徒</sup>

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>公侯</rb><rt>kong-ʽeo</rt><rb>虽然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>弗管</rb><rt>feh-kwun</rt><rb>闲账</rb><rt>ʽæn-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼罗</rb><rt>Nyi-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇个人</rb><rt>keh-go-nying</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>也是</rb><rt>yia-z</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>坏勒</rb><rt>w̆a-leh</rt><rb>呒天理</rb><rt>m-tʽin-li</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲其</rb><rt>kʽô-gyi</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>万众人</rb><rt>væn-cong-nying</rt><rb>打煞</rb><rt>tang-sah</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>廿年</rb><rt>Nyiæn-nyin</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>陀密相</rb><rt>Do-mih-siang</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>信差</rb><rt>sing-tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>充军</rb><rt>cʽong-kyüing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拔摩</rb><rt>Bah-mo</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>海岛</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Domitianus，英语化为 Domitian，现译为<u>图密善</u>。<u>罗马</u>皇帝之一</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Πάτμος，又译为<u>帕特莫斯</u>。海岛名，在<u>希腊</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>越</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>越多</rb><rt>yüih-to</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 98 <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大拉象</rb><rt>Da-lah-ziang</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>帝</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>庇推尼</rb><rt>Pi-tʽe-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-tôh</rt><rb>奏文</rb><rt>tseo-veng</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>行勒</rb><rt>ʽang-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>广</rb><rt>kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>庙宇里</rb><rt>miao-yü-li</rt><rb>拜菩萨个</rb><rt>pa-bu-sah-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 上奏。此处指<u>比提尼亚与本都行省</u>的省长<u>小普林尼</u>和皇帝<u>图拉真</u>的书信</sup>

「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乡方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一般人</rb><rt>ih-pæn-nying</rt><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt><rb>黑早</rb><rt>heh-tsao</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt><rb>做𡍲</rb><rt>tso-tʽah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>唱</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt><rb>曲子</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ts</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>失约</rb><rt>shih-iah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐带拢</rb><rt>zo-tæn-long</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>一厨</rb><rt>ih-djü</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>？」

<ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>其话</rb><rt>gyi-wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>杀个</rb><rt>sah-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>弗少</rb><rt>feh-siao</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 170 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天摇</rb><rt>tʽin-yiao</rt><rb>地动</rb><rt>di-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>动勒</rb><rt>dong-leh</rt><rb>厉害个</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奏文</rb><rt>tseo-veng</rt><rb>皇上</rb><rt>Wông-zông</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>邪教</rb><rt>zia-kyiao</rt><rb>行勒</rb><rt>ʽang-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>广</rb><rt>kwông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>挑唆</rb><rt>tʽiao-so</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>做到</rb><rt>tso-tao</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>上谕</rb><rt>zông-yü</rt><rb>批落来</rb><rt>pʽi-lôh-læ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗必</rb><rt>feh-pih</rt><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有了</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若讲</rb><rt>ziah-kông</rt><rb>天摇</rb><rt>tʽin-yiao</rt><rb>地动</rb><rt>di-dong</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>吃𢞕煞</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>安安</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>稳稳</rb><rt>w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djoh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒罪</rb><rt>m-ze</rt><rb>呒过</rb><rt>m-ko</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安敦</rb><rt>En-teng</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Aurelius，现译为<u>奥勒留</u>。<u>罗马</u>皇帝之一，《后汉书》称其为<u>大秦</u>王<u>安敦</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>总而言之</rb><rt>Tsong-r-yin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby> 300 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>杀掉个</rb><rt>sah-diao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>数都数弗遍</rb><rt>su-tu-su-feh-pin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>只是介</rb><rt>tsih-z-ka</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>越发</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>多起来</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>直等</rb><rt>dzih-teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>贡旦定</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>登位</rb><rt>teng-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>入耶稣教</rb><rt>jih-Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>庙宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>假菩萨</rb><rt>kô-bu-sah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪名个</rb><rt>ze-ming-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从此之后</rb><rt>Dzong-tsʽ-ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>统性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>算是</rb><rt>sön-z</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>lôh-væn</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>弗多</rb><rt>feh-to</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>中国</rb><rt>Cong-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>汉</u>朝</rb><rt>Hen-dziao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>和尚</rb><rt>ʽo-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汉</rb><rt>Hen</rt><rb>明帝</rb><rt>Ming-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聘请</rb><rt>pʽing-tsʽing</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自从</rb><rt>z-dzong</rt><rb>佛教</rb><rt>Veh-kyiao</rt><rb>进</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>中国</rb><rt>Cong-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>毛毛</rb><rt>mao-mao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 2000 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>历朝个</rb><rt>Lih-dziao-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>紧</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>作兴</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>佛个</rb><rt>Veh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>三分之一</rb><rt>sæn-feng-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>而并且</rb><rt>r-ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三分</rb><rt>sæn-feng</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>都计</rb><rt>tu-kyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有口</rb><rt>yiu-kʽeo</rt><rb>无心个</rb><rt>vu-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比弗来个</rb><rt>pi-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>出个</rb><rt>cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>小小管</rb><rt>siao-siao-kun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>推扳</rb><rt>tʽe-pæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乡方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>别</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>刻忖个</rb><rt>kʽeh-tsʽeng-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>统性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>抄灭</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>坏人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>信差</rb><rt>Sing-tsʽa</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt><rb>低微</rb><rt>ti-vi</rt><rb>出身个</rb><rt>cʽih-sing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>行教</rb><rt>ʽang-kyiao</rt><rb>一连</rb><rt>ih-lin</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>朝代</rb><rt>dziao-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>看起来</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>行开去</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恶像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗得知</rb><rt>feh-teh-cü</rt><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt></ruby> 300 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>兴旺了</rb><rt>hying-wông-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>强横</rb><rt>gyiang-wang</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>属国</rb><rt>joh-koh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>前头个</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>真神</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>岂难道</rb><rt>kyʽi-næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>武艺子</rb><rt>vu-nyih-ts</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做勒到个</rb><rt>tso-leh-tao-go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>此地</rb><rt>tsʽ-di</rt><rb>释教</rb><rt>Sih-kyiao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>道教</rb><rt>Dao-kyiao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>衰败</rb><rt>sæ-ba</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看起来</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>中国</rb><rt>Cong-koh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>庉弗得</rb><rt>deng-feh-teh</rt><rb>二三百</rb><rt>nyi-sæn-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 也许；可能</sup>
