<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>坏了</rb><rt>w̆a-de</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>心焦了</rb><rt>Sing-tsiao-de</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>嬉头</rb><rt>hyi-deo</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懂</rb><rt>tong</rt><rb>理性</rb><rt>li-sing</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>德行</rb><rt>teh-ʽang</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>奥古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>耶稣教个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>抲法</rb><rt>kʽô-fah</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>缺点</rb><rt>kyʽüih-tin</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>贡旦定</rb><rt>Kong-tæn-ding</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>释教</rb><rt>Sih-kyiao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>行开</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ</rt><rb>比起来</rb><rt>pi-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？

## <ruby><rb>第七</rb><rt>Di-Tsʽih</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>真神</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>罚咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-liao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>尽头个</rb><rt>zing-deo-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其该管</rb><rt>gyi-kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>人</rb><rt>Lo-mô-nying</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗能够</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>灭进</rb><rt>mih-tsing</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>属国</rb><rt>joh-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>统性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>归拨</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>北头</rb><rt>poh-deo</rt><rb>蛮人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>乱进来</rb><rt>lön-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>统性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>弗能够</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>灭进</rb><rt>mih-tsing</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>只会</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>拜服</rb><rt>pa-voh</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>入教</rb><rt>jih-kyiao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>教门</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>越发</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>行开去</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>终于</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>全</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>欧罗巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奥古士督</rb><rt>Ao-kwu-z-tôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>顶多</rb><rt>ting-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>极好个</rb><rt>gyih-hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>一代</rb><rt>ih-dæ</rt><rb>一代</rb><rt>ih-dæ</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>推扳</rb><rt>tʽe-pæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>蛮人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>乱进个</rb><rt>lön-tsing-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>读书</rb><rt>doh-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>原是</rb><rt>Nyün-z</rt><rb>只要</rb><rt>tsih-iao</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>圣经</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>行开去</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>变做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>光明</rb><rt>kwông-ming</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>印版</rb><rt>ing-pæn</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。《<ruby><rb>圣经</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>抄本</rb><rt>tsʽao-peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>读</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>读弗出</rb><rt>doh-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>话弗来</rb><rt>wô-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>邪开去</rb><rt>zia-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教头</rb><rt>kyiao-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>哄骗</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>差朵</rb><rt>tsʽô-tô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>马太传</rb><rt>Mô-tʽæ-djün</rt></ruby>》XVI，16–19 <ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>–</rt><rb>说错</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>稣</u>封</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>得</u>到</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt><rb>世</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>间上传</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>公</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>会上</rb><rt>kong-we</rt><rb>头。<u>彼</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>得</u>死</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt><rb>后，箇</rb><rt>si-ʽeo</rt><rb>个权</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>柄又</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>落拨</rb><rt>djün-lôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>马</u>教</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt><rb>头了</rb><rt>kyiao-deo</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>教头</rb><rt>kyiao-deo</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>办事体</rb><rt>bæn-z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>样色</rb><rt>yiang-seh</rt><rb>弗会</rb><rt>feh-we</rt><rb>弄错</rb><rt>long-tsʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣口</rb><rt>sing-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>数</rb><rt>su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>铁色无二个</rb><rt>tʽih-seh-vu-r-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>Pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>惧惮</rb><rt>gyü-dæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>旗下人</rb><rt>Gyi-ʽô-nying</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>喇嘛僧</rb><rt>La-mô-seng</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教头</rb><rt>kyiao-deo</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>弄权</rb><rt>long-gyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>邪</rb><rt>zia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>地步</rb><rt>di-bu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>圣经</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>笨个</rb><rt>beng-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>圣经</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>一明白</rb><rt>ih-ming-bah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗肯</rb><rt>feh-kʽeng</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt><rb>乡方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>越弄</rb><rt>yüih-long</rt><rb>越弗是</rb><rt>yüih-feh-z</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>经</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>且顾</rb><rt>tsʽia-kwu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>庵堂</rb><rt>en-dông</rt><rb>寺院里</rb><rt>z-yün-li</rt><rb>修行</rb><rt>siu-ʽang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>死过了</rb><rt>si-ko-liao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>忏念</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所害</rb><rt>sô-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉到</rb><rt>deng-tao</rt><rb>毛毛</rb><rt>mao-mao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 1000 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>黑暗个</rb><rt>heh-en-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>天主教</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>错处</rb><rt>tsʽo-cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby> 1000 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>里头个</rb><rt>li-deo-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>非但</rb><rt>fi-dæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>弗必</rb><rt>feh-pih</rt><rb>说</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缺个</rb><rt>kyʽüih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一两样</rb><rt>ih-liang-yiang</rt><rb>要紧点</rb><rt>iao-kying-tin</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>讲了相似</rb><rt>kông-leh-siang-z</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>呒几百年</rb><rt>m-kyi-pah-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真道理</rb><rt>tsing-dao-li</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>变掉</rb><rt>pin-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>独独</rb><rt>doh-doh</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>真神</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing</rt><rb>弗够</rb><rt>feh-keo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>死过了</rb><rt>si-ko-liao</rt><rb>圣人</rb><rt>sing-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 大大地</sup>

<ruby><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>代</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>受难</rb><rt>ziu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗够</rb><rt>feh-keo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>苦楚</rb><rt>kwʽu-tsʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>虚花</rb><rt>hyü-hwô</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>补补凑</rb><rt>pu-pu-tsʽeo</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] 无用的</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 付予更多。现代宁波话说「找凑」</sup>

<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>出身</rb><rt>cʽih-sing</rt><rb>之地</rb><rt>ts-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>罪名个</rb><rt>ze-ming-go</rt><rb>功劳</rb><rt>kong-lao</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>足足个</rb><rt>tsoh-tsoh-go</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下世个</rb><rt>ʽô-si-go</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt><rb>一定</rb><rt>ih-ding</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算数个</rb><rt>sön-su-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>虔心个</rb><rt>gyin-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>往往</rb><rt>w̆ông-w̆ông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>潮</rb><rt>dziao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>特特</rb><rt>deh-deh</rt><rb>为为</rb><rt>we-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>lôh-væn</rt></ruby> 1055 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>土耳基</rb><rt>Tʽu-r-kyi</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>回子</rb><rt>We-ts</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>照咋</rb><rt>tsiao-dza</rt><rb>难为</rb><rt>næn-we</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>欧罗巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>方</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>拢总怏煞</rb><rt>long-tsong-ông-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>救主个</rb><rt>Kyiu-Cü-go</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>弗相信</rb><rt>feh-siang-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>占住间</rb><rt>tsin-djü-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>耶稣教个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao-go</rt><rb>霉</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>起了</rb><rt>kyʽi-leh</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>个打个</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赦</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 现译为<u>土耳其</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>归根</rb><rt>Kwe-keng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>特买</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>国个</rb><rt>koh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>合带拢</rb><rt>ʽeh-tæn-long</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>回回教</rb><rt>We-we-kyiao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt><rb>稣</rb><rt>su</rt></ruby></u> 1096 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>三年</rb><rt>sæn-nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>破进</rb><rt>pʽo-tsing</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>伤勒</rb><rt>sông-leh</rt><rb>阵里个</rb><rt>dzing-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>算起来</rb><rt>sön-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 85,0000。

<ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby> 80 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>该管个</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>回子</rb><rt>We-ts</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>强起来</rb><rt>gyiang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>夺了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>欧罗巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>血心</rb><rt>hyüih-sing</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各处</rb><rt>Kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>担纲</rb><rt>tæn-kông</rt><rb>起兵</rb><rt>kyʽi-ping</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>伐</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>回子</rb><rt>We-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救转</rb><rt>kyiu-cün</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>Veng-mo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>打上</rb><rt>tang-zông</rt><rb>一百多</rb><rt>ih-pah-to</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>冷</rb><rt>lang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>才始</rb><rt>dzæ-s</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>歇手</rb><rt>hyih-siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伤伤掉个</rb><rt>sông-sông-diao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>足足</rb><rt>tsoh-tsoh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>头两</rb><rt>deo-liang</rt><rb>百万</rb><rt>pah-væn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>回子</rb><rt>We-ts</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>算进</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>一口气</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo-kyʽi</rt><rb>颬勒</rb><rt>hô-leh</rt><rb>壁里</rb><rt>pih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>牵</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>空磨</rb><rt>kʽong-mo</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>蚀煞</rb><rt>zih-sah</rt><rb>老本个</rb><rt>lao-peng-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>邪教</rb><rt>zia-kyiao</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黑暗时世</rb><rt>Heh-en-z-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 白费力气</sup>

<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>第二样</rb><rt>di-nyi-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做根脉<sup>\[1\]</sup>个</rb><rt>tso-keng-mah-go</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>亏了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 源头</sup>

<ruby><rb>偷怕</rb><rt>Tʽeo-pʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都计</rb><rt>tu-kyi</rt><rb>弗依</rb><rt>feh-i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>别国个</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>和众</rb><rt>ʽo-cong</rt><rb>抄灭</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>呒几个</rb><rt>m-kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>兵马</rb><rt>ping-mô</rt><rb>用弗着</rb><rt>yüong-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>直等</rb><rt>dzih-teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 1204 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>忖出</rb><rt>tsʽeng-cʽih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>禁</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>各处</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一进</rb><rt>ih-tsing</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天牢</rb><rt>tʽin-lao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>Dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>衙门</rb><rt>ngô-meng</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衔头</rb><rt>ngæn-deo</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>查察官</rb><rt>Dzô-tsʽah-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>查察</rb><rt>Dzô-tsʽah</rt><rb>衙门</rb><rt>ngô-meng</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若有</rb><rt>Ziah-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>半句</rb><rt>pun-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲错</rb><rt>kông-tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>眼眼</rb><rt>ngæn-ngæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>弗对</rb><rt>feh-te</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>查察官</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah-kwun</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连即</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>独门</rb><rt>doh-meng</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>屈打</rb><rt>kyʽüih-tang</rt><rb>成招</rb><rt>dzing-tsiao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>死罪</rb><rt>Si-ze</rt><rb>定落</rb><rt>ding-lôh</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>顶苦个</rb><rt>ting-kwʽu-go</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>周作</rb><rt>tsiu-tsôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>煞</rb><rt>sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抲了去</rb><rt>kʽô-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连即</rb><rt>lin-tsih</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>头绪</rb><rt>deo-jü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>M-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗敢</rb><rt>feh-ken</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>一响</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>自家</rb><rt>zi-kô</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>难保</rb><rt>næn-pao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>查察官</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah-kwun</rt><rb>衔头</rb><rt>ngæn-deo</rt><rb>虽然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>圣人」个</rb><rt>sing-nying-go</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>圣」字</rb><rt>sing-z</rt><rb>加上</rb><rt>kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>圣查察官</rb><rt>sing-dzô-tsʽah-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>老虎</rb><rt>Lao-hwu</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>好看点</rb><rt>hao-kʽen-tin</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>无冤</rb><rt>vu-ün</rt><rb>无故</rb><rt>vu-kwu</rt><rb>冤枉</rb><rt>ün-wông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拼空</rb><rt>pʽing-kʽong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抲了去</rb><rt>kʽô-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>查察衙门</rb><rt>dzô-tsʽah-ngô-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天主教</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>直等</rb><rt>Dzih-teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落凡</rb><rt>lôh-væn</rt></ruby> 1500 <ruby><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>欧罗巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>教门</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt><rb>邪</rb><rt>zia</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>圣经</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>归正</rb><rt>Kwe-tsing</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>地理</rb><rt>Di-li</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>》53、54 <ruby><rb>面</rb><rt>min</rt></ruby>）。

<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>欧罗巴</rb><rt>Eo-lo-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>天主教个</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有<u>比利时</u></rb><rt>tsih-yiu-Pi-li-z</rt></ruby>、<u><ruby><rb>佛兰西</rb><rt>Veh-læn-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西班牙</rb><rt>Si-pæn-yüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>葡萄牙</rb><rt>Bu-dao-yüô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>奥地利亚</rb><rt>Ao-di-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>还有星</rb><rt>wa-yiu-sing</rt><rb>杂箇愣敦</rb><rt>zeh-keh-leng-teng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>亏了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>别乌碎</rb><rt>bih-u-sen</rt><rb>归正个</rb><rt>kwe-tsing-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>有点</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>借过来</rb><rt>tsia-ko-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>教皇个</rb><rt>kyiao-wông-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>推扳</rb><rt>tʽe-pæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>本来</rb><rt>peng-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>兴起来个</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>圣经</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>行开去</rb><rt>ʽang-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>帖撒罗尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt></ruby>》II, 8）。

<sup>\[1\] 现译为<u>奥地利</u></sup>

### <ruby><rb>问个</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>国</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽæ-koh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>北头</rb><rt>Poh-deo</rt><rb>蛮人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>耶稣教</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-kyiao</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>文理</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>几时</rb><rt>Kyi-z</rt><rb>坏了</rb><rt>w̆a-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>弗转</rb><rt>feh-cün</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主教</rb><rt>cü-kyiao</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>暗了</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>长久</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>天主教</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao</rt><rb>错处</rb><rt>tsʽo-cʽü</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>起头个</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>Heh-en</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>Deo</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>仔细</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>话带来</rb><rt>wô-tæn-læ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>样</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>话勒</rb><rt>wô-leh</rt><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>点</rb><rt>tin</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>Dza</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>归正</rb><rt>Kwe-tsing</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>天主教</rb><rt>Tʽin-cü-kyiao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>呒得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>？《<ruby><rb>帖撒罗尼迦</rb><rt>Tʽiah-sah-lo-nyi-kyüô</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话的</rb><rt>wô-tih</rt></ruby>？

## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>八</rb><rt>Pah</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>自从</rb><rt>Z-dzong</rt><rb>北头</rb><rt>Poh-deo</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>蛮人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>征服</rb><rt>tsing-voh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>分了</rb><rt>feng-leh</rt><rb>特买</rb><rt>deh-ma</rt><rb>国生</rb><rt>koh-sang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>弗能够</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>同心</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>协力</rb><rt>yiah-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>节制</rb><rt>tsih-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>日耳曼</rb><rt>Jih-r-mæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使唤</rb><rt>s-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>佛兰西</rb><rt>Veh-læn-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>奥地利亚</rb><rt>Ao-di-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有点</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天理</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>循环</rb><rt>jing-wæn</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天下个</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>早已仔</rb><rt>tsao-yi-ts</rt><rb>吃尽</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>小苦</rb><rt>siao-kwʽu</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>还报</rb><rt>wæn-pao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>三四百年</rb><rt>Sæn-s-pah-nyin</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>别</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>闹热</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>读书</rb><rt>doh-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>归正了</rb><rt>kwe-tsing-liao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>只会</rb><rt>tsih-we</rt><rb>好起来</rb><rt>hao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>差远</rb><rt>tsʽô-yün</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>活被</rb><rt>weh-be</rt><rb>邪教</rb><rt>zia-kyiao</rt><rb>所害</rb><rt>sô-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希腊</u>国</rb><rt>Hyi-lah-koh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗上</rb><rt>feh-zông</rt><rb>弗落个</rb><rt>feh-lôh-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>庵堂</rb><rt>en-dông</rt><rb>寺院</rb><rt>z-yün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>闹热</rb><rt>nao-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和尚</rb><rt>ʽo-zông</rt><rb>尼姑</rb><rt>nyi-kwu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>圣经</rb><rt>Sing-kying</rt></ruby>》，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>把戏</rb><rt>pô-hyi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做出来</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>乡方</rb><rt>hyiang-fông</rt><rb>虽然</rb><rt>se-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>古时</rb><rt>kwu-z</rt><rb>节</rb><rt>tsin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>真神个</rb><rt>Tsing-Jing-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>假个</rb><rt>kô-go</rt><rb>菩萨</rb><rt>bu-sah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>统</rb><rt>Tʽong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>分了</rb><rt>feng-leh</rt><rb>十国生</rb><rt>jih-koh-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>多斯加纳</rb><rt>To-s-kyüô-neh</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>米兰</rb><rt>Mi-læn</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>撒尔的尼亚</rb><rt>Sah-r-tih-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[2\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>摩纳哥</rb><rt>Mo-neh-ko</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>巴尔马</rb><rt>Pô-r-mô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[3\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>摩德拿</rb><rt>Mo-teh-nô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[4\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>卢加</rb><rt>Lu-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[5\]</sup>、<u><ruby><rb>圣马利涅</rb><rt>Sing-Mô-li-nyiah</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[6\]</sup><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十𡍲</rb><rt>jih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby><sup>\[7\]</sup>。

<sup>\[1\] Toscana，现译为<u>托斯卡纳</u></sup>

<sup>\[2\] Sardegna，现译为<u>撒丁</u></sup>

<sup>\[3\] Parma，现译为<u>帕尔马</u></sup>

<sup>\[4\] Modena，现译为<u>摩德纳</u></sup>

<sup>\[5\] Lucca，现译为<u>卢卡</u></sup>

<sup>\[6\] San Marino，现译为<u>圣马力诺</u></sup>

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十国</rb><rt>jih-koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>统性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt></ruby> 12,0000 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 2250,0000。<ruby><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 188 <ruby><rb>介相貌</rb><rt>ka-siang-mao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 平方英里，1 平方英里约等于 2.59 平方公里</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天家</rb><rt>tʽin-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>和暖</rb><rt>ʽo-nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地土</rb><rt>di-tʽu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>厚</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两边</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>世口</rb><rt>shü-kʽeo</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>便当</rb><rt>bin-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>种田</rb><rt>cong-din</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做生意</rb><rt>tso-sang-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>出息</rb><rt>cʽih-sih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>怕弗</rb><rt>pʽô-feh</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕弗</rb><rt>pʽô-feh</rt><rb>富足起来</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>暴虐</rb><rt>bao-nyiah</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>弗能够</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>十分</rb><rt>jih-feng</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt><rb>起来</rb><rt>kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 时代</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 原书残损，推测为「府」字</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>资质</rb><rt>ts-tsih</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>教门</rb><rt>kyiao-meng</rt><rb>邪</rb><rt>zia</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>读书</rb><rt>doh-shü</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>个数个</rb><rt>ko-su-go</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>山水</rb><rt>sæn-se</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>天生成个</rb><rt>tʽin-sang-dzing-go</rt><rb>秀致</rb><rt>siu-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>西边</rb><rt>Si-pin</rt><rb>随尔</rb><rt>ze-ng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>比弗来个</rb><rt>pi-feh-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>丹青</rb><rt>tæn-tsʽing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>笃石头案</rb><rt>tôh-zah-deo-en</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>细巧</rb><rt>si-kyʽiao</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>顶好</rb><rt>ting-hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>别国</rb><rt>Bih-koh</rt><rb>有名头</rb><rt>yiu-ming-deo</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都是</rb><rt>tu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>学来</rb><rt>ʽôh-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做诗个</rb><rt>tso-s-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗少</rb><rt>feh-siao</rt></ruby>。

### <ruby><rb>问个</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<ruby><rb>自从</rb><rt>Z-dzong</rt><rb>北头</rb><rt>Poh-deo</rt><rb>蛮人</rb><rt>mæn-nying</rt><rb>乱进</rb><rt>lön-tsing</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>咋个了</rb><rt>dza-go-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>三四</rb><rt>Sæn-s</rt><rb>百</rb><rt>pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>读书</rb><rt>doh-shü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>比起来</rb><rt>pi-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>现在呢</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ-ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>乡方</rb><rt>Hyiang-fông</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>分了</rb><rt>feng-leh</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>国生</rb><rt>koh-sang</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>Koh-veng</rt><rb>统性</rb><rt>tʽong-sing</rt><rb>咋管</rb><rt>dza-kun</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>统性</rb><rt>Tʽong-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>一块</rb><rt>Ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>生成</rb><rt>sang-dzing</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>兴旺</rb><rt>hying-wông</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>读书</rb><rt>Doh-shü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>细巧</rb><rt>si-kyʽiao</rt><rb>法子</rb><rt>fah-ts</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>？

## <ruby><rb>第</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>Kyiu</rt><rb>章</rb><rt>Tsông</rt></ruby>

1\. <ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-kôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 1,7200 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 273,0000。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>该管个</rb><rt>kæ-kwun-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>国家个</rb><rt>Koh-kô-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大概</rb><rt>da-kæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>教门里</rb><rt>kyiao-meng-li</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>办个</rb><rt>bæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作兴</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>公事</rb><rt>kong-z</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗许</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>印出</rb><rt>ing-cʽih</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>Pao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>生意</rb><rt>Sang-i</rt><rb>冷静</rb><rt>lang-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讨饭</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt><rb>恶多</rb><rt>ôh-to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大概</rb><rt>Da-kæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十国</rb><rt>jih-koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>末等</rb><rt>meh-teng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>京城</rb><rt>Kying-dzing</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>坐来</rb><rt>zo-læ</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>山顶</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt></ruby>《<ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt><rb>默示录</rb><rt>mah-z-loh</rt></ruby>》XVII）。<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>dzing-li</rt><rb>当初子</rb><rt>tông-tsʽu-ts</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五六十万</rb><rt>ng-loh-jih-væn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>庉的</rb><rt>deng-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>巴巴</rb><rt>pô-pô</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>十四五万</rb><rt>jih-s-ng-væn</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>庵堂</rb><rt>en-dông</rt><rb>寺院</rb><rt>z-yün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>男女</rb><rt>nen-nyü</rt><rb>弗作</rb><rt>feh-tsôh</rt><rb>成亲</rb><rt>dzing-tsʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>城里向</rb><rt>dzing-li-hyiang</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>水土</rb><rt>shü-tʽu</rt><rb>弗好</rb><rt>feh-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>cü</rt><rb>顾</rb><rt>kwu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亏了</rb><rt>Kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>别乌碎</rb><rt>bih-u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>常</rb><rt>djông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>日久之长</rb><rt>nyih-kyiu-ts-dziang</rt><rb>慢慢</rb><rt>mæn-mæn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>变勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>空城</rb><rt>kʽong-dzing</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>古时节个</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin-go</rt><rb>皇宫</rb><rt>wông-kong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衙门</rb><rt>ngô-meng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>庙宇</rb><rt>miao-yü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>好看</rb><rt>hao-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>古迹</rb><rt>kwu-tsih</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>也有</rb><rt>Yia-yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>礼拜堂</rb><rt>li-pa-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扎括</rb><rt>tsah-kwah</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>勒</rb><rt>leh</rt><rb>华华</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>丽丽</rb><rt>li-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>精致</rb><rt>tsing-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>礼拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>堂</rb><rt>dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>彼得堂</rb><rt>Pe-teh-dông</rt></ruby><sup>\[2\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>动工</rb><rt>dong-kong</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt><rb>为止</rb><rt>we-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 117 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby> 1600,00000 <ruby><rb>块</rb><rt>kwʽe</rt><rb>番饼</rb><rt>fæn-ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>教皇</rb><rt>Kyiao-wông</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>弗够</rb><rt>feh-keo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>作出</rb><rt>tsôh-cʽih</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>关牒</rb><rt>kwæn-diah</rt></ruby><sup>\[3\]</sup><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>关牒</rb><rt>kwæn-diah</rt><rb>请去间</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyʽi-kæn</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-Wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 装饰</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 圣<u>伯多禄</u>大殿，或称圣<u>彼得</u>大教堂</sup>

<sup>\[3\] 证书。此处指<u>罗马</u>教廷发行的大赦证明书，即后世所谓赎罪券</sup>

2\. <u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西基里</rb><rt>Si-kyi-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>算进</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 4,2000 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 796,0000。<ruby><rb>京城</rb><rt>Kying-dzing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>亏了</rb><rt>kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-kôh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>载来</rb><rt>tsæ-læ</rt><rb>载去</rb><rt>tsæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>便</rb><rt>bin</rt><rb>闹热个</rb><rt>nao-nyih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>本地</rb><rt>peng-di</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>一眼都</rb><rt>ih-ngæn-tu</rt><rb>呒做</rb><rt>m-tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>过海</rb><rt>ko-hæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt><rb>恶多</rb><rt>ôh-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>震勒</rb><rt>cing-leh</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>房子</rb><rt>vông-ts</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>无千大万</rb><rt>vu-tsʽin-da-væn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>压煞</rb><rt>ah-sah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤈦起来</rb><rt>me-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>气</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>气</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一满</rb><rt>ih-mun</rt><rb>压弗住</rb><rt>ah-feh-djü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>震动</rb><rt>cing-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>出去</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>震勒</rb><rt>cing-leh</rt><rb>岔</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>统</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>外乡</rb><rt>nga-hyiang</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>火山</rb><rt>ho-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>差弗多</rb><rt>tsʽô-feh-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>空心个</rb><rt>kʽong-sing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下头</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>炀开了</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-liao</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>炀开了个</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-liao-go</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>流个</rb><rt>liu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>喇𠲎</rb><rt>La-va</rt></ruby><sup>\[1\]</sup>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爆出来</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>流落</rb><rt>liu-lôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>溪坑</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>式势</rb><rt>sih-shü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>厉害个</rb><rt>li-ʽæ-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] lava，现称为熔岩</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有名头</rb><rt>yiu-ming-deo</rt><rb>火山</rb><rt>ho-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>维苏维</rb><rt>Vi-su-vi</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>爆出来</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。140 <ruby><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山脚下</rb><rt>sæn-kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>古迹</rb><rt>kwu-tsih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掘出来</rb><rt>gyüih-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>瓦爿</rb><rt>ngô-bæn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>起过</rb><rt>kyʽi-ko</rt><rb>一边</rb><rt>ih-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>露出来</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>上好斯样<sup>\[2\]</sup>个</rb><rt>zông-hao-s-nyiang-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>庉晌</rb><rt>Deng-zông</rt></ruby> 40 ><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>放炮</rb><rt>fông-pʽao</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> 5\. <u><ruby><rb>撒尔的尼亚</rb><rt>Sah-r-tih-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>海岛</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>出名个</rb><rt>cʽih-ming-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>Koh-veng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 1,5000 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 360,0000。<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>近来</rb><rt>Gying-læ</rt><rb>三四</rb><rt>sæn-s</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>一道</rb><rt>ih-dao</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>公举</rb><rt>kong-kyü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做官</rb><rt>tso-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>公事</rb><rt>kong-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。

6\. <u><ruby><rb>摩纳哥</rb><rt>Mo-neh-ko</rt></ruby></u><sup>\[1\]</sup><ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby> 52 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby> 7000 <ruby><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有一个</rb><rt>yiu-ih-go</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>全靠</rb><rt>jün-kʽao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒尔的尼亚</rb><rt>Sah-r-tih-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文错印为 mô-neh-ko</sup>

7\. <u><ruby><rb>巴尔马</rb><rt>Pô-r-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby> 2200 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 46,5000。

8\. <u><ruby><rb>摩德拿</rb><rt>Mo-teh-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 2000 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 40,0000。

9\. <u><ruby><rb>卢加</rb><rt>Lu-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby> 400 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 16,5000。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>三𡍲</rb><rt>sæn-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>国王个</rb><rt>koh-wông-go</rt></ruby>。

10\. <u><ruby><rb>圣</rb><rt>Sing</rt><rb>马利涅</rb><rt>Mô-li-nyiah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby> 22 <ruby><rb>转方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt><rb>洋里</rb><rt>yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>wu-kʽeo</rt></ruby> 7000，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>教皇</rb><rt>kyiao-wông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>势道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>Keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一眼眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn-ngæn</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立立</rb><rt>lih-lih</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby> 1300 <ruby><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>合国</rb><rt>ʽAh-kôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>听见</rb><rt>tʽing-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。

### <ruby><rb>问个</rb><rt>Meng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>

<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>Wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>服</rb><rt>Voh</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>公事</rb><rt>Kong-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>百姓拉</rb><rt>Pah-sing-lah</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>国</rb><rt>Lo-mô-koh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别国</rb><rt>bih-koh</rt><rb>比起来</rb><rt>pi-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>罗马城</rb><rt>Lo-mô-dzing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>建来</rb><rt>kyin-læ</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>？《<ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt><rb>默示录</rb><rt>Meh-z-loh</rt></ruby>》<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>点着</rb><rt>tin-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>古迹</rb><rt>kwu-tsih</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>礼拜堂</rb><rt>Li-pa-dông</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>彼得堂</rb><rt>Pe-teh-dông</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话的</rb><rt>wô-tih</rt></ruby>？

<u><ruby><rb>那不勒斯</rb><rt>Na-peh-leh-s</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>户口</rb><rt>Wu-kʽeo</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>生意</rb><rt>Sang-i</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>Di-cing</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>Di-cing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>讲究</rb><rt>kông-kyiu</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>维苏维</rb><rt>Vi-su-vi</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>山脚下</rb><rt>Sæn-kyiah-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>古迹</rb><rt>kwu-tsih</rt><rb>掘出来</rb><rt>gyüih-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>以大拿</rb><rt>Yi-da-nô</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>斯当婆里</rb><rt>S-tông-bo-li</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？

<ruby><rb>讲到</rb><rt>Kông-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多斯加纳</rb><rt>To-s-kyüô-neh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话的</rb><rt>wô-tih</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>米兰</rb><rt>Mi-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>韦内色</rb><rt>We-ne-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>德利士底</rb><rt>Teh-li-z-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>撒尔的尼亚</rb><rt>Sah-r-tih-nyi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以大利</rb><rt>Yi-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>摩纳哥</rb><rt>Mo-neh-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>巴尔马</rb><rt>Pô-r-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<u><ruby><rb>卢加</rb><rt>Lu-Kyüô</rt></ruby></u>？<u><ruby><rb>摩德拿</rb><rt>Mo-teh-nô</rt></ruby></u>？<u><ruby><rb>圣马利涅</rb><rt>Sing-Mô-li-nyiah</rt></ruby></u>？
